lib/presentation/providers/room_provider.dart:

import 'dart:async';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:unila_aqi/data/repositories/room_repository.dart';
import 'package:unila_aqi/core/services/socket_service.dart';
import 'package:unila_aqi/core/services/storage_service.dart';
import '../../data/models/room.dart';

class RoomProvider with ChangeNotifier {
  final RoomRepository _roomRepository = RoomRepository();
  final SocketService _socketService = SocketService();
  final StorageService _storageService = StorageService();

  static const String _cacheKey = 'cache_dashboard_rooms';

  // FIXED: Auto-refresh UI tiap 1 menit.
  // Backend tetap update DB tiap 10 detik — frontend hanya fetch ulang
  // dari API setiap 60 detik, bukan live socket.
  static const Duration _autoRefreshInterval = Duration(minutes: 1);

  List<Room> _rooms = [];
  List<Room> _filteredRooms = [];
  String _selectedBuilding = 'Semua Gedung';
  String _sortBy = 'Terbaru';
  String _searchQuery = '';

  bool _isLoading = false;
  bool _hasError = false;
  String _errorMessage = '';
  bool _socketConnected = false;
  bool _isRefreshing = false;

  DateTime _lastUpdate = DateTime.now();

  StreamSubscription? _socketSubscription;
  // FIXED: timer baru untuk auto-refresh 60 detik.
  Timer? _autoRefreshTimer;

  List<Room> get rooms => _filteredRooms;
  List<Room> get allRooms => _rooms;
  bool get isLoading => _isLoading;
  bool get isRefreshing => _isRefreshing;
  bool get hasError => _hasError;
  String get errorMessage => _errorMessage;
  String get selectedBuilding => _selectedBuilding;
  String get sortBy => _sortBy;
  String get searchQuery => _searchQuery;
  bool get socketConnected => _socketConnected;
  DateTime get lastUpdate => _lastUpdate;

  List<String> get buildings {
    final buildingSet = <String>{'Semua Gedung'};
    for (final room in _rooms) {
      if (room.buildingName.isNotEmpty) {
        buildingSet.add(room.buildingName);
      }
    }
    return buildingSet.toList();
  }

  Future<void> initSocket() async {
    try {
      final token = await _storageService.getString('auth_token');
      await _socketService.connect(token: token);

      _socketSubscription = _socketService.connectionStream.listen((connected) {
        _socketConnected = connected;
        notifyListeners();
      });

      // FIXED: Tidak subscribe ke dataStream lagi.
      // Data update lewat _autoRefreshTimer (60 detik) atau manual refresh.
      // Socket hanya untuk event struktural (room added/deleted/renamed).

      _setupSocketListeners();
      _startAutoRefreshTimer();

      _socketConnected = _socketService.isConnected;
      await _storageService.setSocketConnected(_socketConnected);

      notifyListeners();
    } catch (e) {
      print('❌ Error initializing socket: $e');
      _socketConnected = false;
    }
  }

  /// FIXED: auto-refresh baru — 1 menit sekali, fetch ulang dari API.
  void _startAutoRefreshTimer() {
    _autoRefreshTimer?.cancel();
    _autoRefreshTimer = Timer.periodic(_autoRefreshInterval, (_) async {
      if (_isRefreshing) return;
      print('🔄 RoomProvider auto-refresh (1 minute): fetching fresh data...');
      await _refreshData();
    });
  }

  // FIXED: guard tipe data agar payload null/rusak tidak memicu TypeError.
  void _handleRoomDeleted(dynamic data) {
    if (data is! Map) return;
    try {
      final map = Map<String, dynamic>.from(data);
      final roomId = map['roomId'];
      final roomName = map['roomName'];

      print('🗑️ Processing room deletion: $roomName ($roomId)');

      final initialLength = _rooms.length;
      _rooms.removeWhere((room) => room.id == roomId);

      if (_rooms.length < initialLength) {
        _lastUpdate = DateTime.now();
        _applyFilters();
        notifyListeners();
        print('✅ Room removed from dashboard: $roomName');
      }
    } catch (e) {
      print('❌ Error handling room deletion: $e');
    }
  }

  // FIXED: guard tipe data agar payload null/rusak tidak memicu TypeError.
  void _handleDashboardRoomUpdated(dynamic data) {
    if (data is! Map) return;
    try {
      final map = Map<String, dynamic>.from(data);
      final action = map['action'];
      final roomData = map['room'];

      if (action != 'created' || roomData == null || roomData is! Map) return;

      final roomMap = Map<String, dynamic>.from(roomData);
      final roomId = roomMap['id']?.toString();
      if (roomId == null) return;

      if (_rooms.any((r) => r.id == roomId)) {
        print('ℹ️ Room already exists in list, skipping: $roomId');
        return;
      }

      final newRoom = Room(
        id: roomId,
        name: roomMap['name']?.toString() ?? 'Unknown',
        buildingId: roomMap['buildingId']?.toString() ?? '',
        buildingName: roomMap['buildingName']?.toString() ?? 'Unknown',
        dataSource: roomMap['dataSource']?.toString() ?? 'simulation',
        iotDeviceId: roomMap['iotDeviceId']?.toString(),
        currentAQI: (roomMap['currentAQI'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 0,
        currentData: RoomData.fromJson(
          roomMap['currentData'] is Map
              ? Map<String, dynamic>.from(roomMap['currentData'])
              : <String, dynamic>{},
        ),
        createdAt: roomMap['createdAt'] != null
            ? DateTime.parse(roomMap['createdAt'].toString())
            : DateTime.now(),
        updatedAt: roomMap['updatedAt'] != null
            ? DateTime.parse(roomMap['updatedAt'].toString())
            : DateTime.now(),
      );

      _rooms.add(newRoom);
      _lastUpdate = DateTime.now();
      _applyFilters();
      notifyListeners();

      print('✅ Room added to dashboard: ${newRoom.name} (${newRoom.buildingName})');
    } catch (e) {
      print('❌ Error handling dashboard-room-updated: $e');
    }
  }

  void _setupSocketListeners() {
    // FIXED: hanya event STRUKTURAL yang di-listen.
    // 'room-update' & 'dashboard-update' (data AQI tiap 10s) TIDAK di-listen
    // lagi — data update via _autoRefreshTimer (60s) atau manual refresh.
    _socketService.on('dashboard-room-updated', _handleDashboardRoomUpdated);
    _socketService.on('room-name-changed', (data) {
      handleRoomNameChanged(data);
    });
    _socketService.on('room-building-changed', (data) {
      handleBuildingNameChanged(data);
    });
    _socketService.on('room-deleted', _handleRoomDeleted);
  }

  void _validateSelectedBuilding() {
    final availableBuildings = buildings;
    if (!availableBuildings.contains(_selectedBuilding)) {
      _selectedBuilding = 'Semua Gedung';
    }
  }

  void joinRoom(String roomId) {
    _socketService.joinRoom(roomId);
    _storageService.addSubscribedRoom(roomId);
  }

  void leaveRoom(String roomId) {
    _socketService.leaveRoom(roomId);
    _storageService.removeSubscribedRoom(roomId);
  }

  Future<void> checkSocketConnection() async {
    final isConnected = await _socketService.checkConnection();
    _socketConnected = isConnected;
    await _storageService.setSocketConnected(isConnected);
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> reconnectSocket() async {
    await initSocket();
  }

  Future<void> loadFromCache() async {
    try {
      final cached = _storageService.getJson(_cacheKey);
      if (cached != null && cached['data'] is List) {
        final list = (cached['data'] as List)
            .map((e) => Room.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic>.from(e as Map)))
            .toList();
        if (list.isNotEmpty && _rooms.isEmpty) {
          _rooms = list;
          _applyFilters();
          _lastUpdate = DateTime.now();
          notifyListeners();
        }
      }
    } catch (_) {
      // cache rusak — abaikan, biarkan loadRooms yang isi
    }
  }

  Future<void> loadRooms() async {
    // Hanya tampilkan spinner jika belum ada data (cache kosong).
    _isLoading = _rooms.isEmpty;
    _hasError = false;
    notifyListeners();

    try {
      if (!_socketConnected) {
        await initSocket();
      }

      _rooms = await _roomRepository.getRooms();
      _applyFilters();
      _lastUpdate = DateTime.now();

      // Simpan ke cache untuk cold-start berikutnya
      try {
        await _storageService.setJson(_cacheKey, {
          'data': _rooms.map((r) => r.toJson()).toList(),
        });
      } catch (_) {}
    } catch (e) {
      _hasError = true;
      _errorMessage = e.toString();
      print('❌ Error loading rooms: $e');
    } finally {
      _isLoading = false;
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  /// Manual refresh — dipakai tombol refresh di Dashboard.
  /// Menampilkan spinner (`_isRefreshing = true`).
  Future<void> refresh() async {
    if (_isRefreshing) return;

    _isRefreshing = true;
    _hasError = false;
    notifyListeners();

    try {
      _rooms = await _roomRepository.getRooms();
      _applyFilters();
      _lastUpdate = DateTime.now();

      try {
        await _storageService.setJson(_cacheKey, {
          'data': _rooms.map((r) => r.toJson()).toList(),
        });
      } catch (_) {}
    } catch (e) {
      _hasError = true;
      _errorMessage = e.toString();
    } finally {
      _isRefreshing = false;
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  /// FIXED: silent background refresh — dipanggil oleh _autoRefreshTimer.
  /// Tidak menampilkan spinner, hanya notify kalau ada perubahan.
  Future<void> _refreshData() async {
    try {
      final newRooms = await _roomRepository.getRooms();

      bool hasChanges = false;
      if (newRooms.length != _rooms.length) {
        hasChanges = true;
      } else {
        for (int i = 0; i < newRooms.length; i++) {
          if (newRooms[i].currentAQI != _rooms[i].currentAQI ||
              newRooms[i].updatedAt != _rooms[i].updatedAt) {
            hasChanges = true;
            break;
          }
        }
      }

      if (hasChanges) {
        _rooms = newRooms;
        _applyFilters();
        _lastUpdate = DateTime.now();
        notifyListeners();

        // Cache diperbarui supaya cold-start berikutnya tampil data terbaru
        try {
          await _storageService.setJson(_cacheKey, {
            'data': _rooms.map((r) => r.toJson()).toList(),
          });
        } catch (_) {}

        print('✅ RoomProvider auto-refresh updated ${_rooms.length} rooms');
      } else {
        print('ℹ️ RoomProvider auto-refresh: no changes');
      }
    } catch (e) {
      print('❌ Error during background refresh: $e');
    }
  }

  void _applyFilters() {
    _validateSelectedBuilding();

    List<Room> result = List.from(_rooms);

    if (_selectedBuilding != 'Semua Gedung') {
      result = result.where((room) => room.buildingName == _selectedBuilding).toList();
    }

    if (_searchQuery.isNotEmpty) {
      final query = _searchQuery.toLowerCase();
      result = result.where((room) =>
        room.name.toLowerCase().contains(query) ||
        room.buildingName.toLowerCase().contains(query)
      ).toList();
    }

    switch (_sortBy) {
      case 'Terbaru':
        result.sort((a, b) => b.updatedAt.compareTo(a.updatedAt));
        break;
      case 'A-Z':
        result.sort((a, b) => a.name.compareTo(b.name));
        break;
      case 'AQI Terbaik':
        result.sort((a, b) => a.currentAQI.compareTo(b.currentAQI));
        break;
      case 'AQI Terburuk':
        result.sort((a, b) => b.currentAQI.compareTo(a.currentAQI));
        break;
    }

    _filteredRooms = result;
  }

  void updateBuildingFilter(String building) {
    _selectedBuilding = building;
    _applyFilters();
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void updateSort(String sortBy) {
    _sortBy = sortBy;
    _applyFilters();
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void updateSearchQuery(String query) {
    _searchQuery = query;
    _applyFilters();
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Map<String, List<Room>> get roomsByBuilding {
    final Map<String, List<Room>> result = {};

    for (final room in _filteredRooms) {
      final buildingName = room.buildingName;
      if (!result.containsKey(buildingName)) {
        result[buildingName] = [];
      }
      result[buildingName]!.add(room);
    }

    return result;
  }

  void clearFilters() {
    _selectedBuilding = 'Semua Gedung';
    _sortBy = 'Terbaru';
    _searchQuery = '';
    _applyFilters();
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Room? getRoomById(String roomId) {
    try {
      return _rooms.firstWhere((room) => room.id == roomId);
    } catch (e) {
      return null;
    }
  }

  // FIXED: signature diubah ke dynamic + guard tipe.
  void handleBuildingNameChanged(dynamic data) {
    if (data is! Map) return;
    try {
      final map = Map<String, dynamic>.from(data);
      final buildingId = map['buildingId'];
      final newBuildingName = map['newBuildingName'];

      print('🔄 Processing building name change: $buildingId -> $newBuildingName');

      bool hasChanges = false;
      for (int i = 0; i < _rooms.length; i++) {
        if (_rooms[i].buildingId == buildingId) {
          final updatedRoom = Room(
            id: _rooms[i].id,
            name: _rooms[i].name,
            buildingId: _rooms[i].buildingId,
            buildingName: newBuildingName ?? _rooms[i].buildingName,
            dataSource: _rooms[i].dataSource,
            iotDeviceId: _rooms[i].iotDeviceId,
            currentAQI: _rooms[i].currentAQI,
            currentData: _rooms[i].currentData,
            createdAt: _rooms[i].createdAt,
            updatedAt: DateTime.now(),
          );

          _rooms[i] = updatedRoom;
          hasChanges = true;

          print('✅ Updated room ${_rooms[i].name}: building name -> $newBuildingName');
        }
      }

      if (hasChanges) {
        _applyFilters();
        _lastUpdate = DateTime.now();
        notifyListeners();
      }
    } catch (e) {
      print('❌ Error handling building name change: $e');
    }
  }

  // FIXED: signature diubah ke dynamic + guard tipe.
  void handleRoomNameChanged(dynamic data) {
    if (data is! Map) return;
    try {
      final map = Map<String, dynamic>.from(data);
      final roomId = map['roomId'];
      final newName = map['newName'];
      final oldName = map['oldName'];
      final buildingName = map['buildingName'];

      print('🔄 Processing room name change: $oldName -> $newName ($roomId)');

      final roomIndex = _rooms.indexWhere((room) => room.id == roomId);

      if (roomIndex != -1) {
        final updatedRoom = Room(
          id: _rooms[roomIndex].id,
          name: newName ?? _rooms[roomIndex].name,
          buildingId: _rooms[roomIndex].buildingId,
          buildingName: buildingName ?? _rooms[roomIndex].buildingName,
          dataSource: _rooms[roomIndex].dataSource,
          iotDeviceId: _rooms[roomIndex].iotDeviceId,
          currentAQI: _rooms[roomIndex].currentAQI,
          currentData: _rooms[roomIndex].currentData,
          createdAt: _rooms[roomIndex].createdAt,
          updatedAt: DateTime.now(),
        );

        _rooms[roomIndex] = updatedRoom;
        _applyFilters();
        _lastUpdate = DateTime.now();
        notifyListeners();

        print('✅ Room name updated: $oldName -> $newName');
      } else {
        print('⚠️ Room not found in local list: $roomId');
        _refreshData();
      }
    } catch (e) {
      print('❌ Error handling room name change: $e');
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _autoRefreshTimer?.cancel();
    _socketSubscription?.cancel();
    _socketService.disconnect();
    _socketService.off('dashboard-room-updated');
    _socketService.off('room-deleted');
    _socketService.off('room-name-changed');
    _socketService.off('room-building-changed');
    super.dispose();
  }
}

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
lib/presentation/widgets/chart/history_chart.dart:

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:fl_chart/fl_chart.dart';
import 'package:intl/intl.dart';
import 'package:unila_aqi/core/services/api_service.dart';
import 'package:unila_aqi/core/utils/helpers.dart';
import 'package:unila_aqi/data/models/sensor_data.dart';

class HistoryChart extends StatefulWidget {
  final String roomId;
  final String roomName;
  final String buildingName;

  const HistoryChart({
    super.key,
    required this.roomId,
    required this.roomName,
    required this.buildingName,
  });

  @override
  _HistoryChartState createState() => _HistoryChartState();
}

class _HistoryChartState extends State<HistoryChart> {
  // State variables
  List<SensorData> _historyData = [];
  DateTime _selectedDate = DateTime.now();
  String _selectedParameter = 'aqi';
  bool _isLoading = false;
  String _error = '';
  final ScrollController _scrollController = ScrollController();

  // FOV config — 8 titik per viewport
  static const int _pointsInView = 8;
  static const double _containerPadding = 16.0;

  // Parameter options
  final List<String> _parameters = [
    'aqi',
    'pm25',
    'pm10',
    'co2',
    'temperature',
    'humidity'
  ];
  final Map<String, String> _parameterLabels = {
    'aqi': 'AQI',
    'pm25': 'PM2.5',
    'pm10': 'PM10',
    'co2': 'CO₂',
    'temperature': 'Suhu',
    'humidity': 'Kelembaban',
  };

  // Color for each parameter
  final Map<String, Color> _parameterColors = {
    'aqi': Colors.blue,
    'pm25': Colors.red,
    'pm10': Colors.orange,
    'co2': Colors.green,
    'temperature': Colors.purple,
    'humidity': Colors.teal,
  };

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _loadHistoryData();
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 300), () {
        if (_scrollController.hasClients) {
          _scrollController.animateTo(
            _scrollController.position.maxScrollExtent,
            duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 500),
            curve: Curves.easeOut,
          );
        }
      });
    });
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _scrollController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _loadHistoryData() async {
    if (!mounted) return;

    final hadData = _historyData.isNotEmpty;

    setState(() {
      // Spinner hanya saat belum ada data sama sekali
      _isLoading = !hadData;
      _error = '';
    });

    bool failed = false;
    bool isNetworkFailure = false;

    try {
      final dateStr =
          '${_selectedDate.year}-${_selectedDate.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}-${_selectedDate.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';

      final response =
          await ApiService().getSensorHistory(widget.roomId, dateStr);

      if (!mounted) return;

      if (response is Map && response['success'] == true) {
        final List<dynamic> data = response['data'] ?? [];
        final parsed = data
            .map((json) =>
                SensorData.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic>.from(json)))
            .toList();

        // Jika backend balas sukses tapi kosong → kosongkan (tanggal memang tidak ada data)
        setState(() {
          _historyData = parsed;
        });
      } else {
        failed = true;
        final errStr =
            (response is Map) ? (response['error']?.toString() ?? '') : '';
        isNetworkFailure = Helpers.isNetworkError(errStr);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      failed = true;
      isNetworkFailure = Helpers.isNetworkError(e);
    }

    if (!mounted) return;

    if (failed) {
      // ❗ TIDAK generate sample baru.
      // Data lama (_historyData) tetap dipakai.
      // UI error penuh hanya muncul kalau memang belum pernah ada data.
      if (!hadData) {
        setState(() {
          _error = isNetworkFailure
              ? 'Tidak dapat memuat data grafik karena server tidak dapat dihubungi.'
              : 'Gagal memuat data grafik.';
        });
      }

      if (isNetworkFailure) {
        Helpers.showServerError(context);
      } else {
        Helpers.showSnackBar(
          context,
          'Gagal memuat data grafik',
          isError: true,
        );
      }
    }

    if (mounted) {
      setState(() => _isLoading = false);
    }
  }

  // FIXED: bungkus dengan try-catch. showDatePicker dapat melempar
  // (mis. context tidak valid saat widget sudah unmounted).
  Future<void> _selectDate(BuildContext context) async {
    try {
      final DateTime? picked = await showDatePicker(
        context: context,
        initialDate: _selectedDate,
        firstDate: DateTime.now().subtract(const Duration(days: 30)),
        lastDate: DateTime.now(),
      );

      if (picked != null && picked != _selectedDate) {
        setState(() {
          _selectedDate = picked;
        });
        await _loadHistoryData();
      }
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('❌ Date picker error: $e');
      if (mounted) {
        Helpers.showSnackBar(
          context,
          'Gagal membuka pemilih tanggal',
          isError: true,
        );
      }
    }
  }

  List<FlSpot> _getDataPoints() {
    final spots = <FlSpot>[];

    for (int i = 0; i < _historyData.length; i++) {
      final data = _historyData[i];
      final x = i.toDouble();

      double y;
      switch (_selectedParameter) {
        case 'aqi':
          y = data.aqi.toDouble();
          break;
        case 'pm25':
          y = data.pm25;
          break;
        case 'pm10':
          y = data.pm10;
          break;
        case 'co2':
          y = data.co2;
          break;
        case 'temperature':
          y = data.temperature;
          break;
        case 'humidity':
          y = data.humidity;
          break;
        default:
          y = 0;
      }

      spots.add(FlSpot(x, y));
    }

    return spots;
  }

  // FIXED: interval diubah dari 2 → 1 agar getTitlesWidget dipanggil
  // untuk SETIAP titik. Penyaringan label dilakukan di dalam widget
  // berdasarkan minute == 0 (jam genap: 14:00, 15:00, 16:00).
  //
  // Dengan cara ini, label tetap muncul dengan benar meskipun titik
  // pertama bukan berada di menit :00 (mis. chart dibuka saat 14:30,
  // maka titik pertama adalah 14:30 → label pertama di 15:00).
  SideTitles _getBottomTitles() {
    return SideTitles(
      showTitles: true,
      reservedSize: 30,
      interval: 1,
      getTitlesWidget: (value, meta) {
        // Hanya untuk nilai x yang berupa bilangan bulat (indeks valid)
        final index = value.toInt();
        if (value != index.toDouble()) return const SizedBox.shrink();
        if (index < 0 || index >= _historyData.length) {
          return const SizedBox.shrink();
        }

        final data = _historyData[index];
        final hour = data.timestamp.hour;
        final minute = data.timestamp.minute;

        // FIXED: hanya tampilkan label di jam genap (minute == 0).
        // Titik di menit :30 (14:30, 15:30, ...) tidak diberi label.
        if (minute != 0) return const SizedBox.shrink();

        final hourLabel = hour.toString().padLeft(2, '0');
        return Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 8.0),
          child: Text(
            '$hourLabel:00',
            style: const TextStyle(
              fontSize: 10,
              color: Colors.grey,
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _buildChart() {
    final spots = _getDataPoints();
    final color = _parameterColors[_selectedParameter]!;

    // FIXED: FOV 8 titik per viewport.
    //   pointSpacing = (lebar layar − padding container) / 8
    //   chartWidth   = jumlah titik × pointSpacing
    //   minimal      = lebar viewport (agar chart tidak lebih sempit dari layar)
    //
    // Contoh pada layar 400px:
    //   availableViewport = 400 − 32 = 368
    //   pointSpacing      = 368 / 8 = 46px
    //   48 titik (24 jam) → chartWidth = 48 × 46 = 2208px
    //   viewport 368px    → menampilkan 368/46 = 8 titik
    final screenWidth = MediaQuery.of(context).size.width;
    final availableViewport =
        screenWidth - (_containerPadding * 2);
    final pointSpacing = availableViewport / _pointsInView;

    final calculatedWidth = _historyData.length * pointSpacing;
    final actualChartWidth = calculatedWidth > availableViewport
        ? calculatedWidth
        : availableViewport;

    return Container(
      height: 350,
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(_containerPadding),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.05),
            blurRadius: 10,
            offset: const Offset(0, 4),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Text(
                'Grafik ${_parameterLabels[_selectedParameter]}',
                style: const TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 16,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                ),
              ),
              Container(
                padding:
                    const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: color.withOpacity(0.1),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                ),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    Container(
                      width: 12,
                      height: 12,
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: color,
                        shape: BoxShape.circle,
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 4),
                    Text(
                      _parameterLabels[_selectedParameter]!,
                      style: TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 12,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                        color: color,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          Expanded(
            child: SingleChildScrollView(
              controller: _scrollController,
              scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
              child: Container(
                width: actualChartWidth,
                child: LineChart(
                  LineChartData(
                    gridData: FlGridData(
                      show: true,
                      drawVerticalLine: true,
                      drawHorizontalLine: false,
                      horizontalInterval: 20,
                      getDrawingHorizontalLine: (value) {
                        return const FlLine(
                          color: Colors.transparent,
                          strokeWidth: 0,
                        );
                      },
                      getDrawingVerticalLine: (value) {
                        if (value % 2 == 0) {
                          return FlLine(
                            color: Colors.grey.withOpacity(0.1),
                            strokeWidth: 0.5,
                          );
                        }
                        return const FlLine(
                          color: Colors.transparent,
                          strokeWidth: 0,
                        );
                      },
                    ),
                    titlesData: FlTitlesData(
                      show: true,
                      rightTitles: const AxisTitles(
                        sideTitles: SideTitles(showTitles: false),
                      ),
                      topTitles: const AxisTitles(
                        sideTitles: SideTitles(showTitles: false),
                      ),
                      bottomTitles: AxisTitles(
                        sideTitles: _getBottomTitles(),
                      ),
                      leftTitles: const AxisTitles(
                        sideTitles: SideTitles(showTitles: false),
                      ),
                    ),
                    borderData: FlBorderData(show: false),
                    minX: 0,
                    maxX: _historyData.length > 0
                        ? (_historyData.length - 1).toDouble()
                        : 0,
                    minY: _getMinY(),
                    maxY: _getMaxY(),
                    lineBarsData: [
                      LineChartBarData(
                        spots: spots,
                        isCurved: true,
                        color: color,
                        barWidth: 2.5,
                        isStrokeCapRound: true,
                        dotData: FlDotData(
                          show: true,
                          getDotPainter:
                              (spot, percent, barData, index) {
                            return FlDotCirclePainter(
                              radius: 3.5,
                              color: color,
                              strokeWidth: 2,
                              strokeColor: Colors.white,
                            );
                          },
                        ),
                        belowBarData: BarAreaData(
                          show: true,
                          gradient: LinearGradient(
                            begin: Alignment.topCenter,
                            end: Alignment.bottomCenter,
                            colors: [
                              color.withOpacity(0.3),
                              color.withOpacity(0.1),
                              Colors.transparent,
                            ],
                            stops: const [0.0, 0.5, 1.0],
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                    lineTouchData: LineTouchData(
                      enabled: true,
                      touchTooltipData: LineTouchTooltipData(
                        tooltipBgColor: Colors.white,
                        tooltipRoundedRadius: 8,
                        tooltipPadding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                        fitInsideHorizontally: true,
                        fitInsideVertically: true,
                        maxContentWidth:
                            MediaQuery.of(context).size.width * 0.7,
                        getTooltipItems:
                            (List<LineBarSpot> touchedSpots) {
                          return touchedSpots
                              .map((spot) {
                            final index = spot.spotIndex;
                            if (index < 0 ||
                                index >= _historyData.length) {
                              return null;
                            }

                            final data = _historyData[index];
                            final hour = data.timestamp.hour;
                            final minute = data.timestamp.minute;
                            final time =
                                '${hour.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}:${minute.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';

                            final values = {
                              'AQI': '${data.aqi}',
                              'PM2.5':
                                  '${data.pm25.toStringAsFixed(1)} μg/m³',
                              'PM10':
                                  '${data.pm10.toStringAsFixed(1)} μg/m³',
                              'CO₂': '${data.co2.round()} ppm',
                              'Suhu':
                                  '${data.temperature.toStringAsFixed(1)}°C',
                              'Kelembaban': '${data.humidity.round()}%',
                            };

                            return LineTooltipItem(
                              'Waktu: $time\n\n${values.entries.map((e) => '${e.key}: ${e.value}').join('\n')}',
                              const TextStyle(
                                color: Colors.black87,
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                                fontSize: 12,
                              ),
                            );
                          })
                              .where((element) => element != null)
                              .cast<LineTooltipItem>()
                              .toList();
                        },
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  // FIXED: tetap pertahankan padding 10% di atas & di bawah agar
  // spike (lonjakan mendadak) tetap MUAT di dalam FOV chart.
  // Contoh: data normal AQI 40-60, spike 350 → maxY = 350 × 1.1 = 385,
  // sehingga spike terlihat penuh tanpa terpotong.
  double _getMinY() {
    if (_historyData.isEmpty) return 0.0;

    double minValue = double.infinity;
    for (var data in _historyData) {
      double value;
      switch (_selectedParameter) {
        case 'aqi':
          value = data.aqi.toDouble();
          break;
        case 'pm25':
          value = data.pm25;
          break;
        case 'pm10':
          value = data.pm10;
          break;
        case 'co2':
          value = data.co2;
          break;
        case 'temperature':
          value = data.temperature;
          break;
        case 'humidity':
          value = data.humidity;
          break;
        default:
          value = 0.0;
      }
      if (value < minValue) minValue = value;
    }

    return minValue - (minValue * 0.1);
  }

  double _getMaxY() {
    if (_historyData.isEmpty) return 100.0;

    double maxValue = double.negativeInfinity;
    for (var data in _historyData) {
      double value;
      switch (_selectedParameter) {
        case 'aqi':
          value = data.aqi.toDouble();
          break;
        case 'pm25':
          value = data.pm25;
          break;
        case 'pm10':
          value = data.pm10;
          break;
        case 'co2':
          value = data.co2;
          break;
        case 'temperature':
          value = data.temperature;
          break;
        case 'humidity':
          value = data.humidity;
          break;
        default:
          value = 0.0;
      }
      if (value > maxValue) maxValue = value;
    }

    return maxValue + (maxValue * 0.1);
  }

  Widget _buildParameterSelector() {
    return Container(
      height: 50,
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 16),
      child: ListView.builder(
        scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
        itemCount: _parameters.length,
        itemBuilder: (context, index) {
          final parameter = _parameters[index];
          final isSelected = _selectedParameter == parameter;
          final color = _parameterColors[parameter]!;

          return GestureDetector(
            onTap: () {
              setState(() {
                _selectedParameter = parameter;
              });
            },
            child: Container(
              margin: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 8),
              padding:
                  const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 8),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: isSelected ? color : color.withOpacity(0.1),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(25),
                border: Border.all(
                  color: isSelected ? color : Colors.transparent,
                  width: 2,
                ),
              ),
              child: Row(
                children: [
                  Container(
                    width: 12,
                    height: 12,
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: isSelected ? Colors.white : color,
                      shape: BoxShape.circle,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                  Text(
                    _parameterLabels[parameter]!,
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 14,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                      color: isSelected ? Colors.white : color,
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildDateSelector() {
    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 16),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.05),
            blurRadius: 8,
            offset: const Offset(0, 2),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
        children: [
          Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              const SizedBox(height: 4),
              Text(
                DateFormat('EEEE, dd MMMM yyyy').format(_selectedDate),
                style: const TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 16,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          Row(
            children: [
              IconButton(
                onPressed: () => _selectDate(context),
                icon: const Icon(Icons.calendar_today),
                tooltip: 'Pilih tanggal lain',
              ),
              IconButton(
                onPressed: _isLoading ? null : _loadHistoryData,
                icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh),
                tooltip: 'Refresh data',
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        _buildDateSelector(),
        _buildParameterSelector(),
        if (_isLoading)
          Container(
            height: 350,
            alignment: Alignment.center,
            child: const CircularProgressIndicator(),
          )
        else if (_error.isNotEmpty && _historyData.isEmpty)
          Container(
            height: 350,
            alignment: Alignment.center,
            child: Column(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
              children: [
                const Icon(
                  Icons.error_outline,
                  size: 48,
                  color: Colors.red,
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),
                Text(
                  _error,
                  textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                  style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.red),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),
                ElevatedButton(
                  onPressed: _loadHistoryData,
                  child: const Text('Coba Lagi'),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          )
        else if (_historyData.isEmpty)
          Container(
            height: 350,
            alignment: Alignment.center,
            child: const Column(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
              children: [
                Icon(
                  Icons.insert_chart_outlined,
                  size: 48,
                  color: Colors.grey,
                ),
                SizedBox(height: 16),
                Text(
                  'Tidak ada data history untuk tanggal ini',
                  style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          )
        else
          _buildChart(),
      ],
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../../data/models/room.dart';
import 'room_card.dart';

class BuildingSection extends StatelessWidget {
  final String buildingName;
  final List<Room> rooms;
  final Function(Room)? onRoomTap;
  
  const BuildingSection({
    super.key,
    required this.buildingName,
    required this.rooms,
    this.onRoomTap,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 24),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          
          // Building Header
// Building Header
Container(
  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 8),
  decoration: BoxDecoration(
    color: Theme.of(context).primaryColor.withOpacity(0.1),
    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
  ),
  child: IntrinsicHeight(
    child: Row(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch, 
      children: [
        // ✅ Ikon sekarang di tengah secara vertikal
        Align(
          alignment: Alignment.center, // Ubah dari topLeft ke center
          child: const Icon(Icons.location_city, size: 20), // Padding dihapus
        ),
        const SizedBox(width: 8),
        
        // Nama gedung (bisa wrap ke bawah)
        Expanded(
          child: Text(
            buildingName,
            style: const TextStyle(
              fontSize: 18,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
            ),
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(width: 8),
        
        // Chip jumlah ruangan (tetap di tengah)
        Align(
          alignment: Alignment.center, 
          child: Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 2),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Theme.of(context).primaryColor,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
            ),
            child: Text(
              '${rooms.length} ruangan',
              style: const TextStyle(
                fontSize: 12,
                color: Colors.white,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    ),
  ),
),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          // Rooms List
          ListView.separated(
            physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
            shrinkWrap: true,
            itemCount: rooms.length,
            separatorBuilder: (context, index) => const SizedBox(height: 12),
            itemBuilder: (context, index) {
              final room = rooms[index];
              return RoomCard(
                room: room,
                onTap: () {
                  if (onRoomTap != null) {
                    onRoomTap!(room);
                  }
                },
              );
            },
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

lib/presentation/widgets/common/drawer.dart:

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';
import '../../../presentation/providers/auth_provider.dart';
import '../../../core/constants/colors.dart';

class AppDrawer extends StatelessWidget {
  final bool isAdmin;
  final VoidCallback? onDashboardTap;
  final VoidCallback? onBuildingsTap;
  final VoidCallback? onRoomsTap;
  final VoidCallback? onDevicesTap;
  final VoidCallback? onProfileTap;
  final VoidCallback? onLogoutTap;
  final VoidCallback? onHelpTap; 
  
  
  const AppDrawer({
    super.key,
    required this.isAdmin,
    this.onDashboardTap,
    this.onBuildingsTap,
    this.onRoomsTap,
    this.onDevicesTap,
    this.onProfileTap,
    this.onLogoutTap,
    this.onHelpTap,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final authProvider = Provider.of<AuthProvider>(context, listen: false);
    final currentUser = authProvider.currentUser;
    
    return Drawer(
      child: Column(
        children: [
          // Header
                    // Header
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 48, 16, 24),
            // --- PERUBAHAN DI SINI ---
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              gradient: AppColors.primaryGradient,
              boxShadow: [
                BoxShadow(
                  color: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.25),
                  blurRadius: 12,
                  offset: const Offset(0, 4),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            // -------------------------
            child: Column(
              children: [
                Container(
                  width: 100,
                  height: 100,
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    // --- PERUBAHAN DI SINI (Menyesuaikan gaya Profil Admin) ---
                    color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.2), 
                    shape: BoxShape.circle,
                    border: Border.all(
                      color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.5),
                      width: 3,
                    ),
                    // -------------------------
                  ),
                  child: Padding(

  Widget _buildMenuItem({
    required IconData icon,
    required String label,
    VoidCallback? onTap,
  }) {
    return ListTile(
      leading: Icon(icon),
      title: Text(
        label,
        style: const TextStyle(
          fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
        ),
      ),
      onTap: onTap,
    );
  }

      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
        child: Column(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
          children: [
            // Label
            Text(
              label,
              style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 10,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.9),
              ),
            ),

            const SizedBox(height: 4),

            // Value and Unit
            Row(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.baseline,
              textBaseline: TextBaseline.alphabetic,
              children: [
                Text(
                  value,
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 16,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                    color: Colors.white,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 2),
                Text(
                  unit,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 9,
                    color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.8),
                  ),
                ),

            ),

            const SizedBox(height: 6),

            // Status chip

                status.length > 10 ? '${status.substring(0, 10)}...' : status,

                  color: Colors.white,
                ),
                overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                maxLines: 1,
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

lib/presentation/widgets/common/server_status_indicator.dart

import 'dart:async';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../../core/services/server_status_service.dart';

class ServerStatusIndicator extends StatefulWidget {
  final Duration pollInterval;
  final double size;

  const ServerStatusIndicator({
    super.key,
    this.pollInterval = const Duration(seconds: 10),
    this.size = 10,
  });

  static Future<bool> checkNow() => ServerStatusService().checkNow();

  @override
  State<ServerStatusIndicator> createState() => _ServerStatusIndicatorState();
}

class _ServerStatusIndicatorState extends State<ServerStatusIndicator> {
  final ServerStatusService _service = ServerStatusService();
  Timer? _timer;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    // Check pertama kali instan
    _service.checkNow();
    // Polling berkala sebagai fallback (refresh button tetap jalan
    // lewat static helper).
    _timer = Timer.periodic(widget.pollInterval, (_) => _service.checkNow());
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _timer?.cancel();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return ValueListenableBuilder<bool?>(
      valueListenable: _service.status,
      builder: (context, value, _) {
        final Color color;
        if (value == null) {
          color = Colors.grey;
        } else if (value == true) {
          color = Colors.green;
        } else {
          color = Colors.red;
        }

        return Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 6),
          child: Center(
            child: AnimatedContainer(
              duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
              width: widget.size,
              height: widget.size,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: color,
                shape: BoxShape.circle,
                boxShadow: [
                  BoxShadow(
                    color: color.withOpacity(0.55),
                    blurRadius: 6,
                    spreadRadius: 1,
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

const http = require('http');
const url = require('url');

// ============================================================
// ISPU Calculator (sama persis dengan backend)
// ============================================================
const PM25_BREAKPOINTS = [
  { ispuLow: 0,   ispuHigh: 50,  concLow: 0,   concHigh: 55  },
  { ispuLow: 51,  ispuHigh: 100, concLow: 56,  concHigh: 150 },
  { ispuLow: 101, ispuHigh: 200, concLow: 151, concHigh: 250 },
  { ispuLow: 201, ispuHigh: 300, concLow: 251, concHigh: 350 },
  { ispuLow: 301, ispuHigh: 500, concLow: 351, concHigh: 500 },
];
const PM10_BREAKPOINTS = [
  { ispuLow: 0,   ispuHigh: 50,  concLow: 0,   concHigh: 50  },
  { ispuLow: 51,  ispuHigh: 100, concLow: 51,  concHigh: 150 },
  { ispuLow: 101, ispuHigh: 200, concLow: 151, concHigh: 350 },
  { ispuLow: 201, ispuHigh: 300, concLow: 351, concHigh: 420 },
  { ispuLow: 301, ispuHigh: 500, concLow: 421, concHigh: 500 },
];

function calculateISPU(conc, bps) {
  if (conc == null || isNaN(conc) || conc < 0) return 0;
  for (const bp of bps) {
    if (conc <= bp.concHigh) {
      const range = bp.concHigh - bp.concLow;
      if (range === 0) return bp.ispuHigh;
      const ispu = ((bp.ispuHigh - bp.ispuLow) / range) * (conc - bp.concLow) + bp.ispuLow;
      return Math.round(Math.max(0, Math.min(500, ispu)));
    }
  }
  return 500;
}

const calculateISPUFromPM25 = (pm25) => calculateISPU(pm25, PM25_BREAKPOINTS);
const calculateISPUFromPM10 = (pm10) => calculateISPU(pm10, PM10_BREAKPOINTS);

// ============================================================
// Time-based ranges
// ============================================================

function getTimeSlot(hour) {
  if (hour >= 6  && hour < 8)  return '06-08';
  if (hour >= 8  && hour < 12) return '08-12';
  if (hour >= 12 && hour < 16) return '12-16';
  if (hour >= 16 && hour < 18) return '16-18';
  if (hour >= 18 && hour < 20) return '18-20';
  return '20-06';
}

function randomInRange(min, max, mean) {
  const mode = Math.max(min, Math.min(max, mean ?? (min + max) / 2));
  const u = Math.random();
  const f = (mode - min) / (max - min);
  if (u < f) return min + Math.sqrt(u * (max - min) * (mode - min));
  return max - Math.sqrt((1 - u) * (max - min) * (max - mode));
}

// In-memory storage
const registeredDevices = new Map();
const deviceStates = new Map();
const secondDataCache = new Map();

// ============================================================
// Anomaly simulation (SAMA dengan backend dataGenerator)
// 92% normal / 5% spike (8-17x) / 3% error
// ============================================================
function applyAnomaly(pm25, pm10, co2, temperature, humidity) {
  const roll = Math.random();

  if (roll < 0.05) {
    const subRoll = Math.random();
    let multiplier;
    if (subRoll < 0.60) {
      multiplier = 8 + Math.random() * 2;   // 8-10x
    } else if (subRoll < 0.90) {
      multiplier = 10 + Math.random() * 3;  // 10-13x
    } else {
      multiplier = 13 + Math.random() * 4;  // 13-17x
    }

    return {
      isAnomaly: true,
      anomalyType: 'spike',
      pm25: pm25 * multiplier,
      pm10: pm10 * multiplier,
      co2: co2 * (1.5 + Math.random()),
      temperature,
      humidity,
    };
  }

  if (roll < 0.08) {
    return {
      isAnomaly: true,
      anomalyType: 'error',
      pm25: -1, pm10: -1, co2: -1, temperature: -1, humidity: -1,
    };
  }

  return {
    isAnomaly: false,
    anomalyType: null,
    pm25, pm10, co2, temperature, humidity,
  };
}

// Helper: Generate data per 10 detik
function generate10SecondBasedData(deviceId) {
  const now = new Date();
  const currentSecond = Math.floor(now.getTime() / 1000);
  const current10SecondBlock = Math.floor(currentSecond / 10);
  const hour = now.getHours();
  const minute = now.getMinutes();
  const second = now.getSeconds();

  // Inisialisasi state device
  if (!deviceStates.has(deviceId)) {
    const hash = deviceId.split('').reduce((acc, c) => acc + c.charCodeAt(0), 0);
    deviceStates.set(deviceId, {
      roomType: hash % 4,
      prevValues: null,
      last10SecondBlock: current10SecondBlock - 1,
      updateCounter: 0,
    });
    console.log(`📊 Init device ${deviceId}:`, {
      roomType: ['Kelas', 'Laboratorium', 'Perpustakaan', 'Aula'][deviceStates.get(deviceId).roomType],
    });
  }

  const state = deviceStates.get(deviceId);

  // Cache 10 detik
  if (secondDataCache.has(deviceId)) {
    const cached = secondDataCache.get(deviceId);
    if (cached.block10 === current10SecondBlock) {
      return cached.data;
    }
  }

  const slot = getTimeSlot(hour);
  const r = TIME_RANGES[slot];

  const rawPm25 = randomInRange(r.pm25.min, r.pm25.max, r.pm25.mean);
  const rawPm10 = randomInRange(r.pm10.min, r.pm10.max, r.pm10.mean);
  const rawCo2  = randomInRange(r.co2.min,  r.co2.max,  r.co2.mean);
  const rawTemp = randomInRange(r.temperature.min, r.temperature.max, r.temperature.mean);
  const rawRh   = randomInRange(r.humidity.min,    r.humidity.max,    r.humidity.mean);

  const alpha = 0.4;
  const prev = state.prevValues || {
    pm25: rawPm25, pm10: rawPm10, co2: rawCo2,
    temperature: rawTemp, humidity: rawRh,
  };
  const pm25 = prev.pm25 * (1 - alpha) + rawPm25 * alpha;
  const pm10 = prev.pm10 * (1 - alpha) + rawPm10 * alpha;
  const co2  = prev.co2  * (1 - alpha) + rawCo2  * alpha;
  const temperature = prev.temperature * (1 - alpha) + rawTemp * alpha;
  const humidity    = prev.humidity    * (1 - alpha) + rawRh   * alpha;

  // Update state (JANGAN feed anomali ke state — supaya spike transien 1 tick)
  state.prevValues = { pm25, pm10, co2, temperature, humidity };
  state.updateCounter++;
  state.last10SecondBlock = current10SecondBlock;

  // FIXED: apply anomaly — hasilnya TIDAK mengubah state.prevValues,
  // sehingga spike hanya muncul 1 tick dan hilang pada tick berikutnya.
  const anomalous = applyAnomaly(pm25, pm10, co2, temperature, humidity);

  // Hitung AQI
  let finalAQI, finalCategory;
  if (anomalous.anomalyType === 'error') {
    finalAQI = -1;
    finalCategory = 'error';
  } else {
    const ispuPM25 = calculateISPUFromPM25(anomalous.pm25);
    const ispuPM10 = calculateISPUFromPM10(anomalous.pm10);
    finalAQI = Math.max(ispuPM25, ispuPM10);

    if (finalAQI <= 50) finalCategory = 'baik';
    else if (finalAQI <= 100) finalCategory = 'sedang';
    else if (finalAQI <= 200) finalCategory = 'tidak_sehat';
    else if (finalAQI <= 300) finalCategory = 'sangat_tidak_sehat';
    else finalCategory = 'berbahaya';
  }

  const sensorData = {
    deviceId,
    timestamp: now.toISOString(),
    block10: current10SecondBlock,
    secondsInBlock: second % 10,
    updateCounter: state.updateCounter,
    aqi: finalAQI,
    pm25: parseFloat(anomalous.pm25.toFixed(1)),
    pm10: parseFloat(anomalous.pm10.toFixed(1)),
    co2: Math.round(anomalous.co2),
    temperature: parseFloat(anomalous.temperature.toFixed(1)),
    humidity: Math.round(anomalous.humidity),
    category: finalCategory,
    roomType: ['Kelas', 'Laboratorium', 'Perpustakaan', 'Aula'][state.roomType],
    timeSlot: slot,
    factors: { hour, minute, second },
    isAnomaly: anomalous.isAnomaly,
    anomalyType: anomalous.anomalyType,
  };

  secondDataCache.set(deviceId, {
    block10: current10SecondBlock,
    data: sensorData,
    timestamp: now,
  });

  if (anomalous.isAnomaly) {
    console.log(
      `🚨 ${deviceId} ANOMALY (${anomalous.anomalyType}): AQI=${finalAQI} (${finalCategory}), PM2.5=${sensorData.pm25}`
    );
  } else {
    console.log(
      `🔄 ${deviceId} [${slot}] AQI=${finalAQI}, PM2.5=${sensorData.pm25}, PM10=${sensorData.pm10}`
    );
  }

  return sensorData;
}

// Clean old cache setiap 30 detik
setInterval(() => {
  const now = Date.now();
  const currentBlock = Math.floor(now / (10 * 1000));

  for (const [deviceId, cache] of secondDataCache.entries()) {
    if (cache.block10 < currentBlock - 3) {
      secondDataCache.delete(deviceId);
    }
  }
}, 30000);

function setCorsHeaders(res) {
  res.setHeader('Access-Control-Allow-Origin', '*');
  res.setHeader('Access-Control-Allow-Methods', 'GET, POST, PUT, DELETE, OPTIONS');
  res.setHeader('Access-Control-Allow-Headers', 'Content-Type');
}

function sendJson(res, statusCode, data) {
  res.writeHead(statusCode, {
    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*'
  });
  res.end(JSON.stringify(data, null, 2));
}

const server = http.createServer((req, res) => {
  const parsedUrl = url.parse(req.url, true);
  const pathname = parsedUrl.pathname;
  const method = req.method;

  const now = new Date();
  console.log(`[${now.getHours().toString().padStart(2, '0')}:${now.getMinutes().toString().padStart(2, '0')}:${now.getSeconds().toString().padStart(2, '0')}] ${method} ${pathname}`);

  setCorsHeaders(res);

  if (method === 'OPTIONS') {
    res.writeHead(200);
    res.end();
    return;
  }

  let body = '';
  req.on('data', chunk => { body += chunk.toString(); });

  req.on('end', () => {
    let requestData = {};
    if (body && req.headers['content-type'] === 'application/json') {
      try {
        requestData = JSON.parse(body);
      } catch (e) {
        return sendJson(res, 400, { success: false, message: 'Invalid JSON' });
      }
    }

    try {
      if (pathname === '/health' && method === 'GET') {
        const now = new Date();
        const currentSecond = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
        const current10SecondBlock = Math.floor(currentSecond / 10);
        const nextChangeInSeconds = 10 - (now.getSeconds() % 10);

        sendJson(res, 200, {
          status: 'ok',
          service: 'UNILA AQI IoT Simulator - 10 SECOND UPDATES',
          version: '5.0.0',
          currentTime: now.toISOString(),
          current10SecondBlock,
          secondsInCurrentBlock: now.getSeconds() % 10,
          nextUpdateIn: nextChangeInSeconds,
          devices: registeredDevices.size,
          cacheSize: secondDataCache.size,
          features: [
            '10-second updates',
            'Predictable patterns',
            'Anomaly simulation (5% spike, 3% error)',
            'Room type variations',
            'Time-of-day patterns'
          ]
        });
        return;
      }

      if (pathname === '/register' && method === 'POST') {
        const { deviceId, deviceName, location, roomType } = requestData;

        if (!deviceId) {
          return sendJson(res, 400, { success: false, message: 'deviceId is required' });
        }

        let roomTypeNum = 0;
        if (roomType === 'laboratory' || roomType === 'lab') roomTypeNum = 1;
        else if (roomType === 'library') roomTypeNum = 2;
        else if (roomType === 'auditorium' || roomType === 'hall') roomTypeNum = 3;

        registeredDevices.set(deviceId, {
          deviceId,
          deviceName: deviceName || `Device-${deviceId.substring(0, 8)}`,
          location: location || 'UNILA Campus',
          roomType: roomTypeNum,
          registeredAt: new Date(),
          lastSeen: new Date(),
          status: 'active',
          updatePattern: '10-second-based'
        });

        console.log(`✅ Device registered: ${deviceId}`);

        sendJson(res, 200, {
          success: true,
          message: 'Device registered successfully',
          device: registeredDevices.get(deviceId),
        });
        return;
      }

      if (pathname.startsWith('/data/') && method === 'GET') {
        const deviceId = pathname.split('/')[2];

        if (!deviceId) {
          return sendJson(res, 400, { success: false, message: 'Device ID is required' });
        }

        if (!registeredDevices.has(deviceId)) {
          const hash = deviceId.split('').reduce((acc, char) => acc + char.charCodeAt(0), 0);
          const roomType = hash % 4;

          registeredDevices.set(deviceId, {
            deviceId,
            deviceName: `Auto-${deviceId}`,
            location: 'UNILA Campus',
            roomType,
            registeredAt: new Date(),
            lastSeen: new Date(),
            status: 'active',
            updatePattern: '10-second-based-auto'
          });

          console.log(`✅ Auto-registered: ${deviceId}`);
        } else {
          const device = registeredDevices.get(deviceId);
          device.lastSeen = new Date();
          registeredDevices.set(deviceId, device);
        }

        const sensorData = generate10SecondBasedData(deviceId);

        const now = new Date();
        const secondsRemaining = 10 - (now.getSeconds() % 10);

        const response = {
          success: true,
          message: 'Sensor data (updates every 10 seconds)',
          device: registeredDevices.get(deviceId),
          data: sensorData,
          metadata: {
            source: 'UNILA AQI IoT Simulator - 10 SECOND UPDATES',
            updateFrequency: 'Every 10 seconds',
            currentBlock: sensorData.block10,
            secondsInCurrentBlock: sensorData.secondsInBlock,
            nextUpdateInSeconds: secondsRemaining,
            isAnomaly: sensorData.isAnomaly,
            anomalyType: sensorData.anomalyType,
            units: {
              pm25: 'μg/m³',
              pm10: 'μg/m³',
              co2: 'ppm',
              temperature: '°C',
              humidity: '%'
            }
          }
        };

        sendJson(res, 200, response);
        return;
      }

      if (pathname.startsWith('/status/') && method === 'GET') {
        const deviceId = pathname.split('/')[2];

        if (!deviceId) {
          return sendJson(res, 400, { success: false, message: 'Device ID is required' });
        }

        const deviceInfo = registeredDevices.get(deviceId);
        if (!deviceInfo) {
          return sendJson(res, 404, { success: false, message: 'Device not found' });
        }

        const now = new Date();
        const lastSeen = new Date(deviceInfo.lastSeen);
        const secondsSinceLastSeen = (now - lastSeen) / 1000;
        const currentSecond = Math.floor(now.getTime() / 1000);
        const current10SecondBlock = Math.floor(currentSecond / 10);
        const secondsRemaining = 10 - (now.getSeconds() % 10);

        const state = deviceStates.get(deviceId);
        const cache = secondDataCache.get(deviceId);

        sendJson(res, 200, {
          success: true,
          device: deviceInfo,
          status: {
            online: secondsSinceLastSeen < 120,
            lastSeen: deviceInfo.lastSeen,
            secondsSinceLastSeen: Math.round(secondsSinceLastSeen),
            current10SecondBlock,
            secondsInCurrentBlock: now.getSeconds() % 10,
            nextUpdateInSeconds: secondsRemaining,
            hasCachedData: !!cache,
            cachedBlock: cache ? cache.block10 : null,
            stateInfo: state ? {
              roomType: ['Kelas', 'Laboratorium', 'Perpustakaan', 'Aula'][state.roomType],
              updateCounter: state.updateCounter,
            } : 'Not initialized'
          }
        });
        return;
      }

      if (pathname.startsWith('/force-update/') && method === 'POST') {
        const deviceId = pathname.split('/')[2];

        if (!deviceId) {
          return sendJson(res, 400, { success: false, message: 'Device ID is required' });
        }

        secondDataCache.delete(deviceId);

        if (deviceStates.has(deviceId)) {
          const state = deviceStates.get(deviceId);
          state.last10SecondBlock = -1;
        }

        console.log(`🔄 Force update triggered for ${deviceId}`);

        sendJson(res, 200, {
          success: true,
          message: 'Force update triggered',
          deviceId,
          note: 'Next request will generate new 10-second-based data'
        });
        return;
      }

      if (pathname.startsWith('/set-base/') && method === 'POST') {
        const deviceId = pathname.split('/')[2];
        const { pm25, pm10, co2, temperature, humidity, roomType } = requestData;

        if (!deviceId) {
          return sendJson(res, 400, { success: false, message: 'Device ID is required' });
        }

        if (!deviceStates.has(deviceId)) {
          deviceStates.set(deviceId, {
            basePM25: 20, basePM10: 40, baseCO2: 550,
            baseTemp: 23, baseHumidity: 50, roomType: 0,
            trendPM25: 0, trendCO2: 0, last10SecondBlock: -1, updateCounter: 0
          });
        }

        const state = deviceStates.get(deviceId);

        if (pm25 !== undefined) state.basePM25 = pm25;
        if (pm10 !== undefined) state.basePM10 = pm10;
        if (co2 !== undefined) state.baseCO2 = co2;
        if (temperature !== undefined) state.baseTemp = temperature;
        if (humidity !== undefined) state.baseHumidity = humidity;
        if (roomType !== undefined) {
          if (roomType === 'laboratory' || roomType === 'lab') state.roomType = 1;
          else if (roomType === 'library') state.roomType = 2;
          else if (roomType === 'auditorium' || roomType === 'hall') state.roomType = 3;
          else state.roomType = 0;
        }

        secondDataCache.delete(deviceId);

        sendJson(res, 200, {
          success: true,
          message: 'Custom base values set',
          deviceId,
          values: {
            basePM25: state.basePM25,
            basePM10: state.basePM10,
            baseCO2: state.baseCO2,
            baseTemp: state.baseTemp,
            baseHumidity: state.baseHumidity,
            roomType: ['Kelas', 'Laboratorium', 'Perpustakaan', 'Aula'][state.roomType]
          }
        });
        return;
      }

      if (pathname.startsWith('/history/') && method === 'GET') {
        const deviceId = pathname.split('/')[2];
        const blocks = parseInt(parsedUrl.query.blocks) || 60;

        if (!deviceId) {
          return sendJson(res, 400, { success: false, message: 'Device ID is required' });
        }

        const now = new Date();
        const currentSecond = Math.floor(now.getTime() / 1000);
        const currentBlock = Math.floor(currentSecond / 10);
        const history = [];

        if (deviceStates.has(deviceId)) {
          const state = deviceStates.get(deviceId);

          for (let i = blocks; i >= 0; i--) {
            const historyBlock = currentBlock - i;
            const historyTime = new Date(now.getTime() - (i * 10 * 1000));

            const updateOffset = state.updateCounter - i;
            const hour = historyTime.getHours();
            const minute = historyTime.getMinutes();

            let timeFactor = 1.0;
            if (hour >= 7 && hour <= 18) timeFactor = 1.2;

            const pm25 = state.basePM25 * timeFactor * (1 + Math.sin(updateOffset * 0.2) * 0.2);
            const pm10 = pm25 * 2;
            const co2 = state.baseCO2 * timeFactor;

            let aqi;
            if (pm25 <= 12.0) aqi = Math.round((pm25 / 12.0) * 50);
            else if (pm25 <= 35.4) aqi = Math.round(51 + ((pm25 - 12.1) / (35.4 - 12.1)) * 49);
            else aqi = Math.round(101 + ((pm25 - 35.5) / (55.4 - 35.5)) * 49);

            history.push({
              timestamp: historyTime.toISOString(),
              block: historyBlock,
              aqi: Math.min(500, aqi),
              pm25: parseFloat(pm25.toFixed(1)),
              pm10: parseFloat(pm10.toFixed(1)),
              co2: Math.round(co2),
              temperature: state.baseTemp + Math.sin(hour * 0.2618 + minute * 0.00436) * 3,
              humidity: state.baseHumidity + Math.cos(hour * 0.2618 + minute * 0.00436) * 10
            });
          }
        }

        sendJson(res, 200, {
          success: true,
          deviceId,
          period: `${blocks * 10} seconds (${blocks} blocks)`,
          currentBlock,
          count: history.length,
          data: history
        });
        return;
      }

      if (pathname === '/devices' && method === 'GET') {
        const devices = Array.from(registeredDevices.values());
        const now = new Date();
        const currentSecond = Math.floor(now.getTime() / 1000);
        const currentBlock = Math.floor(currentSecond / 10);
        const secondsRemaining = 10 - (now.getSeconds() % 10);

        const devicesWithInfo = devices.map(device => {
          const lastSeen = new Date(device.lastSeen);
          const secondsSinceLastSeen = (now - lastSeen) / 1000;
          const state = deviceStates.get(device.deviceId);
          const cache = secondDataCache.get(device.deviceId);

          return {
            ...device,
            roomTypeName: ['Kelas', 'Laboratorium', 'Perpustakaan', 'Aula'][device.roomType || 0],
            status: secondsSinceLastSeen < 120 ? 'online' : 'offline',
            secondsSinceLastSeen: Math.round(secondsSinceLastSeen),
            hasState: !!state,
            updateCounter: state ? state.updateCounter : 0,
            cachedData: !!cache,
            cachedAtBlock: cache ? cache.block10 : null,
            currentBlock,
            nextUpdateIn: secondsRemaining
          };
        });

        sendJson(res, 200, {
          success: true,
          currentBlock,
          currentTime: now.toISOString(),
          secondsInCurrentBlock: now.getSeconds() % 10,
          nextUpdateInSeconds: secondsRemaining,
          totalDevices: devices.length,
          onlineDevices: devicesWithInfo.filter(d => d.status === 'online').length,
          devices: devicesWithInfo
        });
        return;
      }

      sendJson(res, 404, {
        success: false,
        message: 'Endpoint not found',
        path: pathname
      });

    } catch (error) {
      console.error('❌ Server error:', error);
      sendJson(res, 500, {
        success: false,
        message: 'Internal server error',
        error: error.message
      });
    }
  });
});

const PORT = process.env.IOT_PORT || 3002;
const HOST = process.env.IOT_HOST || 'localhost';

server.listen(PORT, HOST, () => {
  console.log('🚀 UNILA AQI IoT Simulator - 10 SECOND UPDATES');
  console.log(`📍 URL: http://${HOST}:${PORT}`);
  console.log(`📡 Health Check: http://${HOST}:${PORT}/health`);

  console.log('\n⏰ FEATURE HIGHLIGHTS:');
  console.log('   ✅ Data BERUBAH SETIAP 10 DETIK');
  console.log('   ✅ Pola berdasarkan waktu (jam, hari)');
  console.log('   ✅ ANOMALY: 5% spike (8-17x), 3% sensor error');
  console.log('   ✅ Cache per 10 detik');
  console.log('   ✅ Room type variations');
  console.log('   ✅ EMA smoothing (alpha=0.4)');

  console.log('\n💡 Contoh untuk Aplikasi AQI:');
  console.log(`  Endpoint: http://${HOST}:${PORT}/data/kelas-h101`);
  console.log(`  Data akan berubah setiap 10 detik`);
  console.log(`  Sekitar 5% request akan menghasilkan AQI 100-500 (spike)`);
  console.log(`  Sekitar 3% request akan menghasilkan AQI=-1 (sensor error)`);
});
